If any <t> in a paragraph changes, that\n    paragraph is considered modified and all descendant <...:linesegarray>\n    elements under that paragraph are removed.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    # Parse XML\n    root = ET.fromstring(xml_text)\n    ns_uri = _namespace_uri(root.tag)\n    if not ns_uri:\n        raise ValueError(\"Could not determine XML namespace URI from root tag\")\n\n    #
